Notably, Phi-4-mini-instruct excels in paraphrasing, Kimi-K2-32b-instruct in span detection, and Gemini-2.5-flash in meaning identification. We believe that our datasets and analyses will provide valuable resources to guide future research in improving LLM comprehension of idiomatic expressions, particularly in Bangla and other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [141] [Lngram v2: Latent N-Gram Memory with Interpretable Discrete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03426)
*Yunao Zheng,Bin Wen,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lngram v2 提出一种可扩展的离散潜在 n-gram 条件记忆机制，通过解耦路由数、记忆维度与主干宽度，并采用上下文感知的 grouped-query attention 读出，在降低参数与激活成本的同时提升或保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 缺少原生查找机制，难以高效识别和复用局部静态模式；Lngram v1 虽引入离散潜在 n-gram 寻址记忆，但其记忆容量与主干宽度耦合，导致扩展时参数量和激活成本过高。

Method: 论文提出 Lngram v2：解耦路由数量、记忆维度和 backbone 宽度；使用上下文感知 grouped-query attention 进行记忆读出，使记忆容量可独立扩展；引入 zero-value Sink 提高读出选择性，并使用 counterfactual surrogate gradients 改善硬离散寻址下的路由可训练性。

Result: 在不同规模视觉-语言模型实验中取得一致改进，并成功扩展到 30B 参数模型；相比 Lngram v1，Lngram v2 显著减少总记忆参数和激活记忆参数，同时保持或提升语言建模性能；离散 ID 还能保留连续隐藏状态中的显著语义结构。

Conclusion: Lngram v2 是一种高效、可扩展的潜在条件记忆机制，不仅提升了模型复用静态模式的能力，还提供了用于分析内部表示的结构化离散地址接口。

Abstract: Transformers lack a native lookup mechanism, requiring repeated dense computation to recognize and reuse local static patterns. Lngram v1 introduces tokenizer-independent conditional memory through discrete latent n-gram addressing, but its memory capacity is coupled with the backbone width, limiting scalability due to high parameter and activation costs. We propose Lngram v2, which decouples the number of routes, memory dimension, and backbone width, and introduces a context-aware grouped-query attention readout to scale memory capacity independently. A zero-value Sink and counterfactual surrogate gradients further improve readout selectivity and routing trainability while preserving hard discrete addressing. Experiments across vision--language models (VLMs) of different scales show consistent improvements, including successful scaling to a 30B-parameter model. Compared with Lngram v1, Lngram v2 substantially reduces both total and activated memory parameters while maintaining or improving language modeling performance. Further analysis shows that its discrete IDs preserve substantial semantic structure of continuous hidden states, enabling semantic recovery from IDs alone and stable ID--semantic associations across datasets. These results establish Lngram v2 as an efficient and scalable latent conditional memory mechanism whose discrete addresses also provide a structured interface for analyzing internal model representations.

</details>


### [142] [Decoupled Analysis-Judging: An Automated Creativity Evaluator Using LLMs in Complex Multi-step Creativity Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03432)
*Xiangyu Wang,Jin Wu,Xiaoyu Li,Chanjin Zheng,Yifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CreaEval 将创造力任务评估拆分为“证据分析”和“基于证据评分”，以降低 LLM-as-a-Judge 在复杂多步任务中的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge 在自动评估创造力任务时容易受到冗长偏差、宽容偏差等影响；在上下文依赖、步骤结构复杂、主观性强且评分范围大的 CGPST 中，这些问题更加突出。现有方法要么依赖任务特定训练，要么直接使用 LLM 评判，难以保证稳定可靠的评估。

Method: 提出 CreaEval：将传统 LLM 评判流程解耦为两个阶段。第一阶段为 Memory-augmented Analysis，由 SoT-LLM 将多步回答转化为结构化评估证据，并引入跨步骤记忆以捕捉步骤间依赖；第二阶段为 Evidence-based Judging，由 Judge-LLM 仅基于提取出的证据进行评分，不直接访问原始回答。

Result: 实验显示，CreaEval 在 CGPST 以及两个经典简单创造力任务上，相比次优基线平均性能提升 22.74%，表明其在复杂与简单创造力评估场景中均具有较好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CreaEval 通过证据化、结构化和记忆增强的分析流程，缓解了 LLM 直接评判中的偏差与不稳定性，为复杂多步创造力任务提供了更可靠的自动评估框架。

Abstract: Automated evaluation of creativity tasks remains challenging for LLM-as-a-Judge, as LLM is susceptible to biases such as verbosity bias and leniency bias. Such limitations are particularly evident in Contextually-Grounded and Procedurally-Structured Tasks (CGPST), a complex multi-step creativity task where inter-step dependencies, highly subjectivity, and wide scoring ranges lead to more unstable and biased judgments. Existing approaches either rely on task-specific training or directly apply LLM-as-a-Judge, both of which struggle to ensure reliable evaluation under such complexity. To bridge these gaps, we propose CreaEval, an automated creativity evaluator for CGPST that decouples typical LLM-as-a-Judge into analysis and judging. Correspondingly, CreaEval involves two critical phases: Memory-augmented Analysis, a SoT-LLM converts multi-step responses into structured evaluation evidence, incorporating cross-step memory; and Evidence-based Judging, a Judge-LLM uses the extracted evidence for judging without accessing raw responses. Comprehensive experiments show that CreaEval achieves an average performance improvement of 22.74% over the second-best baselines across CGPST and two classic simple creativity tasks, demonstrating its generalizability. The code is available at https://github.com/Jaong/CreaEval.

</details>


### [143] [When Retrieval Helps: Selective Retrieval for Single-Turn Mental-Health QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03454)
*Hyunseo Oh,Chong-Kwon Kim,Yoonhyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究心理健康单轮问答中 RAG 何时有益或有害，并提出轻量级选择性检索策略来控制检索带来的质量与安全权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 通常能提升大模型回答的具体性与依据性，但在心理健康问答中，用户问题常包含情绪困扰、治疗顾虑与安全敏感需求，检索并不总是带来正收益，甚至可能降低回答质量或引入额外风险。因此需要判断何时应启用检索。

Method: 作者以三个基于草稿回答的效用维度来刻画检索需求：心理教育需求、应对策略需求和回答具体性，并加入规则化安全触发器；构建包含应对策略、心理教育和安全资源的可控小型指南语料库；使用 MentalChat16K 对指令微调生成器进行 QLoRA 微调，并在 CounselBench-Eval 与 CounselBench-Adv 上比较闭卷、总是检索和选择性检索三种设置。

Result: 实验表明，检索在该领域并非一致有益。总是检索可以提升回答具体性，但会降低整体质量，并带来更多安全敏感失败；选择性检索能在低需求场景保留闭卷模型行为，同时避免无条件检索造成的额外退化。

Conclusion: 在心理健康问答中，是否启用检索应被视为一种安全敏感的控制决策。轻量级选择性检索比无条件检索更适合平衡回答具体性、整体质量和安全性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can improve the specificity and grounding of large language model responses, but its effect is not uniformly beneficial in single-turn mental-health question answering, where user queries often combine emotional distress, treatment concerns, and safety-sensitive needs. We study when retrieval helps or hurts mental-health QA, and whether a lightweight selective retrieval policy can better control this trade-off. We operationalize retrieval need using three draft-conditioned utility dimensions: psychoeducational need, coping need, and response specificity, together with a rule-based safety trigger. Following psychotherapy-grounded RAG systems such as coTherapist, we construct a compact and controllable guideline corpus comprising coping-strategy, psychoeducational, and safety resources. We fine-tune an instruction-tuned generator on MentalChat16K using QLoRA and compare Closed-book, Always Retrieval, and Selective Retrieval settings on CounselBench-Eval and CounselBench-Adv. Experiments show that retrieval is not uniformly beneficial in this domain. Always Retrieval improves specificity but lowers overall quality and introduces additional safety-sensitive failures. Selective Retrieval preserves closed-book behavior for low-need cases while avoiding the additional degradation caused by unconditional retrieval, supporting the view that retrieval activation is a safety-sensitive control decision.

</details>


### [144] [When Users Don't Ask: Benchmarking Context-Driven Memory Retrieval in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03467)
*Wen-Yu Chang,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LOCOMO-CONV，一个面向长程对话代理的会话式记忆评测基准，显示传统 QA 式评测会低估真实对话场景中的检索缺口，尤其在隐式和组合查询上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被用于长程对话代理，记忆系统变得重要；但现有基准多通过 QA 探测评估记忆，难以反映记忆在真实对话中的原位使用效果。

Method: 作者基于 LoCoMo 构建 LOCOMO-CONV，设计四类查询形式：对话式、隐式、反事实、组合式；在五种代表性记忆系统上同时评估检索召回率与端到端回复质量，并分析不同记忆表示与查询改写策略的影响。

Result: 实验发现，会话式框架暴露出 QA 基准忽略的显著检索缺口，隐式与组合查询尤其困难；多维度查询改写能缩小原始轮次记忆的差距，但对抽象式记忆帮助有限；较强检索并不总能转化为更高回复质量；隐式查询中存在“静默 grounding”现象，即记忆改善上下文扎根性但不显式呈现金标准事实。

Conclusion: 论文表明，对话场景下的记忆评估应超越 QA 式探测，关注检索与生成质量的耦合关系；基于推理的记忆扩展/细化是有前景方向，作者还发布 supportive_memory 标注以捕捉对话中有用但超出原始金证据的上下文。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increas- ingly deployed as long-horizon conversational agents, motivating growing interest in mem- ory systems. However, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate memory through QA-style probing rather than in-situ conversational usage. We introduce LOCOMO-CONV, a conversa- tional memory benchmark derived from Lo- CoMo with four query styles: dialog, implicit, counterfactual, and composed. Across five rep- resentative memory systems, we evaluate both retrieval recall and end-to-end response qual- ity. Our experiments show that conversational framing exposes substantial retrieval gaps over- looked by QA benchmarks, especially on im- plicit and composed queries, which multi-facet query rewriting narrows for raw-turn mem- ory but not abstractive memory. We further find that strong retrieval does not fully trans- late into response quality, and that implicit queries exhibit silent grounding, where mem- ory improves contextual grounding without ex- plicitly surfacing the gold fact. These results point to reasoning-based memory elaboration as a promising direction, and we release aux- iliary supportive_memory annotations captur- ing conversationally useful context beyond the original gold evidence.

</details>


### [145] [Pattern Over-Generalization of Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03487)
*Junsik Kim,Kangil Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PogRE，一种面向知识图谱嵌入的关系表示方法，用稠密线性变换与复合操作缓解模式过度泛化问题，并在标准链接预测基准上取得优于现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱嵌入模型虽然能建模对称/反对称、逆关系、组合等推理模式，但常把从单个或少量模式实例中学到的规律无条件推广到所有相关实例，导致模式过度泛化，影响链接预测可靠性。

Method: PogRE 采用稠密线性变换和复合操作来表示关系；理论上分析稠密线性变换如何随观测到的模式三元组数量增加而逐步增强模式的普适性，并证明当观测到 d+1 个线性无关实体后可保证该模式在相关实例上的普遍泛化。

Result: 在三个标准知识图谱链接预测基准数据集上，PogRE 的性能超过已有最先进 KGE 模型；经验结果还表明其能有效降低过度泛化带来的负面影响。

Conclusion: PogRE 通过更可控的关系变换机制，在保持推理模式建模能力的同时缓解过度泛化，是一种简单有效且理论与实验均有支撑的 KGE 方法。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) demonstrates its effectiveness for predicting missing links in knowledge graphs (KGs) by projecting entities and relations into a low-dimensional vector space. It is crucial for KGE models to effectively capture inference patterns (patterns) inherent in KGs, such as symmetry/antisymmetry, inversion and composition. Although recent KGE models exhibit strong capabilities in modeling such diverse patterns, they suffer from inherent limitations stemming from pattern over-generalization, where embeddings learned from only a single pattern instance inevitably generalize that pattern to all related instances, i.e., generalize the pattern universally. To address this issue, we propose PogRE (Pattern Over-Generalization Robust Embedding), a simple but effective method that utilizes dense linear transformations and compound operations for relation representation. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that a dense linear transformation allows a pattern to become progressively universal as more triples are observed in the pattern. Furthermore, after observing d+1 linearly independent entities (d+1 denotes the dimension of entity), the linear transformation guarantees universal generalization of the pattern across all related instances. Experimental results on three standard benchmark datasets show that PogRE outperforms existing state-of-the-art KGE models in link prediction. Moreover, our empirical results indicate that PogRE effectively addresses the negative impact of over-generalization.

</details>


### [146] [Building and Evaluating Fixed-Voice Thai TTS from Synthetic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03502)
*Kunat Pipatanakul,Potsawee Manakul,Warit Sirichotedumrong,Sittipong Sripaisarnmongkol,Pakorn Nathong,Phatrasek Jirabovonvisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用大型语音克隆模型为短参考音色生成合成语音数据，再训练紧凑固定音色学生模型，实现低资源场景下的端侧 TTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源 TTS 部署通常在高推理成本的大型语音克隆模型与需要特定说话人语料的小型固定音色系统之间取舍；作者希望利用短音色参考和合成数据训练小模型，降低部署成本并避免真实说话人语料需求。

Method: 以大型语音克隆模型作为“可编程数据源”，从约 15 秒参考音频生成合成训练集；系统研究文本准备、合成生成、质量过滤、拒绝采样和前端设计对学生模型的影响，并针对分词歧义、声调、姓名/外来词、数字读法及跨语言混读等问题进行评估。

Result: 得到 8200 万参数的 Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge，可在无参考音频情况下进行端侧 TTS；Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy 达 68.2%，暂停精确率 91.4%，泰语/英语 CER 分别为 3.7% 和 1.1%，在若干停顿相关指标上优于教师模型。

Conclusion: 大型教师模型生成的合成数据可有效蒸馏为紧凑固定音色端侧 TTS，但教师错误和过滤策略会影响学生模型覆盖与质量；论文同时开源模型与评测框架以促进相关 TTS 研究。

Abstract: In low-resource settings, deploying TTS typically requires choosing between a large voice-cloning model with costly inference or a compact fixed-voice system that requires a speaker-specific corpus. We study a third route: using a large voice-cloning model as a programmable data source to turn a short voice reference (e.g., 15 seconds) into a compact fixed-voice student trained entirely on synthetic speech. This setting makes pipeline design consequential: teacher errors become training targets, while filtering failed generations can reduce coverage of difficult texts. Thai further introduces challenges from ambiguous word boundaries, lexical tone, names and loanwords, numeric verbalization, and Thai-English code-switching. We study how text preparation, synthetic generation, quality filtering, rejection sampling, and frontend choices affect the resulting student, and where teacher limitations remain. We evaluate CER, Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy, Prosody Pause Accuracy, speaker similarity, and speaking rate. The resulting 82M-parameter model, Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge, enables on-device Thai TTS without reference audio. It achieves 68.2% Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy (85.5% of Gemini 3.1) and 91.4% pause precision, outperforming its OmniVoice teacher (89.9%) and reaching 94.8% of Gemini 3.1. It also achieves the lowest pause-placement error and intra-word pause rates among the three systems, and 3.7% and 1.1% CER on Thai and English, respectively. We open-source the model and evaluation framework for Thai TTS development.

</details>


### [147] [Lost in Reordering: Structural Sensitivity of Multilingual LLMs under Semantics-Preserving Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03511)
*Karthika Nhayakkat,Rajat Verma,Maharaj Brahma,Vetcha Gnana Mahesh,Maunendra Sankar Desarkar,Ganesh Ramakrishnan,Rohit Saluja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IndicReStruct 基准，研究多语言大模型在印地语和马拉雅拉姆语中面对保持语义不变的结构扰动时的数学推理鲁棒性，发现模型性能显著下降，说明其缺乏对句法表层变化的组合不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在多语言推理任务上表现较强，但其是否能在语义不变、句法结构变化的输入下保持稳定，尤其是在词序较自由的语言中，仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 作者基于 GSM8K 构建 IndicReStruct 数据集，包含 GSM8K-Reordered（受约束成分重排）和 GSM8K-Voice（主动/被动语态转换）两个变体；在印地语和马拉雅拉姆语上评测六个先进大模型与多种提示策略，并结合定性错误分析和残差流激活修补进行机制可解释性实验。

Result: 实验显示，所有模型在结构扰动输入下的数学推理性能均出现一致且显著下降；错误常与实体—数量对齐被破坏有关；激活修补表明中间 Transformer 层对恢复推理能力贡献最大。

Conclusion: 当前多语言大模型对表层句法实现高度敏感，面对语义等价但结构不同的输入时缺乏稳健的组合不变性，说明其多语言推理能力仍存在重要鲁棒性短板。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong multilingual reasoning performance, yet their robustness to semantics-preserving structural variation remains underexplored, particularly for relatively free word-order languages. We investigate the structural sensitivity of multilingual LLMs using two linguistically grounded perturbation settings in Hindi and Malayalam: constrained constituent reordering and active-passive voice transformation. We introduce a benchmark dataset IndicReStruct, with two variants, GSM8K-Reordered and GSM8K-Voice, constructed from GSM8K while preserving semantic meaning. Across six state-of-the-art LLMs and multiple prompting strategies, we observe consistent and significant degradation in mathematical reasoning performance under structurally perturbed inputs. To further understand these failures, we perform qualitative error analysis and mechanistic interpretability experiments using residual-stream activation patching. Our analyses show that reasoning failures frequently arise from disruptions in entity-quantity alignment and that intermediate transformer layers contribute most strongly toward reasoning restoration. Overall, our findings suggest that current multilingual LLMs remain highly sensitive to surface syntactic realization and lack robust compositional invariance under structurally different but semantically equivalent inputs.

</details>


### [148] [Language, Language Models, and What We're Talking About](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03577)
*Malvina Nissim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文讨论语言模型并非单纯技术产物，而是由训练数据中的语言世界塑造；以意大利语模型为例，反思翻译数据、合成数据与非自然测试数据对“语言模型究竟建模何种语言”的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型常被视为技术系统，但其输出与能力深受训练语料、微调语料和评测语料的语言性质影响。作者希望重新关注 NLP 是否仍关心真实语言，以及不同用途下语言模型应产生何种语言。

Method: 文章采取概念分析与批判性讨论的方法，以意大利语语言模型为例，考察经翻译数据、合成数据训练和进一步人工策展后的模型，以及在同样非自然数据上测试这些模型所带来的认识论问题。

Result: 作者指出，这类模型未必能被简单称为“意大利语模型”或一般意义上的“语言模型”；其语言表现可能更多反映工程目标、数据加工过程和评测设置，而非自然语言本身。

Conclusion: 需要区分作为技术产品的语言模型与作为语言研究工具的语言模型。只有明确模型用途和目标语言形态后，才能回答我们希望模型生成何种语言；不同答案可能对应不同类型的语言模型，前景也未必悲观。

Abstract: Language models are commonly discussed as technical artefacts, but they are obviously shaped by the linguistic worlds conveyed by data during their training. Using Italian language models as evidence, I want to bring attention to the nature of the systems which result from training and specialising models on translated and synthetic data, and further curating them, and to the meaning of testing them on equally unnatural data. Are these eventually models of Italian? Are they models of language? Does NLP still care about language? These questions yield another, more concrete question: what language do we actually want language models to produce? I argue that this question cannot be answered if we do not first consider a clearer distinction between language models designed as technical products and language models designed as tools for studying language itself. The answers then might be diverse, the languages we are talking about might be diverse, and the picture might not be as pessimistic as we fear.

</details>


### [149] [How Far Can Synthetic Data Take Thai OCR?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03595)
*Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究哪些合成 OCR 监督因素能有效迁移到真实泰文文档，并提出仅用合成页面、无需真实 OCR 标签适配的 Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可大规模提供精确标签，但所谓“真实性”混杂了源域、页面上下文、字体、空间结构与字形变化等因素。作者希望拆解这些因素，明确哪些因素真正促进合成到真实泰文 OCR 的迁移。

Method: 作者构建受控的文档重建流程，分别改变非文本上下文、字体多样性、二维版面结构、真实手写字形、源域匹配等因素，并在页级与裁剪级训练设置下，于印刷体和手写泰文文档上评估。基于实验结论，使用 45,723 个合成页面对 0.9B 参数 PaddleOCR-VL-1.6 进行适配，得到 Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero。

Result: 非文本上下文影响不稳定；字体多样性、二维结构与真实手写字形能提升迁移。源域匹配效果依赖训练粒度：页级训练中域内重建接近真实印刷监督，median CER 为 1.82% 对 1.31%；裁剪级训练中域内重建弱于域外重建，15.59% 对 5.52%。最终模型相较基座在印刷页上 CER 从 6.64% 降至 1.24%，在手写与域外数据上从 74.87% 降至 20.55%，并在五个评测集上优于 Typhoon OCR v1 7B。

Conclusion: 合成数据的有效性不取决于笼统的视觉真实性，而取决于字体、结构、字形与训练粒度等可控因素。仅用合成数据训练/适配也能在泰文 OCR 中达到有竞争力的性能，尤其在无真实标注场景下具有实际价值。

Abstract: We investigate what makes synthetic OCR supervision transfer to real Thai documents and use the resulting insights to build Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero, a Thai OCR model adapted without OCR labels from real Thai document pages. Synthetic data provide exact labels at scale, but "realism" conflates source domain, page context, typography, spatial structure, and glyph variation. We disentangle these factors with a controlled document-reconstruction pipeline and evaluate each variant under page- and crop-level training on printed and handwritten Thai documents. Non-text context has little consistent effect, whereas typeface diversity, two-dimensional structure, and real handwriting glyphs improve transfer; moreover, source-domain matching depends on training granularity, with in-domain reconstruction approaching real printed supervision under page-level training (1.82% versus 1.31% median character error rate) but underperforming out-of-domain reconstruction under crop-level training (15.59% versus 5.52%). Guided by these findings, we adapt the 0.9B-parameter PaddleOCR-VL-1.6 into Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero using 45,723 synthetic pages: relative to its base checkpoint, it reduces median character error rate from 6.64% to 1.24% on printed pages and from 74.87% to 20.55% on handwriting and outperforms Typhoon OCR v1 7B on all five evaluation sets, showing that synthetic-only training can be competitive.

</details>


### [150] [KhatianDoc: A Human-Verified Benchmark Diagnosing Multimodal LLM Failure on Bengali Legal Land Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03597)
*Tasmiad Hasan,Arafat Zaman Ratul,Sarker Sadman Saalim,S. M. Shah Nawaz Hossain,Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 KhatianDoc，一个面向孟加拉国 RS Khatian 土地权属记录的多任务基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型是否能识别、转换、抽取并问答其中的专用地籍分数记法；实验显示现有模型在多个关键任务上几乎不具备可靠能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RS Khatian 是大量土地权属与民事案件中的权威记录，其中使用 Ana-Ganda-Kora-Kranti-Til 这种带专用字形的十六进制位置分数体系。该体系缺乏主流字体、OCR 支持和 tokenizer 覆盖，导致现有文档智能系统无法有效处理这类高法律价值的手写档案。

Method: 作者构建 KhatianDoc 基准，数据来自 107 份真实 RS Khatian 记录，包含四项任务：符号识别、十六进制到十进制转换、结构化字段抽取、以及基于 1,634 个问答对的法律文档问答。标注由人工转录，并由土地法从业者验证；数据通过位置化 token 匿名化以保留多跳问答所需的指代关系。作者在固定零样本协议下评测 6 个 8B 到 72B+ 的开源与闭源多模态 LLM。

Result: 实验结果表明，在分层问答集合中有 5 类问题、占 39.3%，所有模型均无法答对；在算术转换任务中，所有输出数值的模型都差于常数均值基线，且精确匹配与近似匹配分数一致，说明问题不是数值近似误差，而是模型输出与真实值失相关。作者还修正了拒答评分 bug，并指出一个元数据指标存在虚高，只能作为上界。

Conclusion: KhatianDoc 揭示的不是一般性能差距，而是现有多模态 LLM 对该类法律地籍文档缺乏基本能力。该基准提供经过验证的真实标注和公开代码数据，可作为未来 OCR、文档理解与法律问答系统在低资源专用符号体系上的评测基础。

Abstract: Land ownership in Bangladesh is recorded in Ana-Ganda-Kora-Kranti-Til, a base-16 positional fraction system with dedicated Unicode glyphs, no mainstream font, and no coverage in any OCR pipeline or tokenizer. The handwritten records that carry these fractions, RS Khatians, are the authoritative title record for millions of parcels and a frequent subject of civil litigation, yet no benchmark has asked whether a machine can read one. We introduce KhatianDoc, a four-task benchmark built from 107 real RS Khatian records from the Vumi (land) Office of Munshiganj, Bangladesh: symbol recognition, base-16-to-decimal conversion, structured field extraction, and legal document question answering over 1,634 QA pairs. Ground truth was transcribed by hand, verified by a land-law practitioner to full agreement, and anonymized through positional tokens that keep the referential distinctions multi-hop questions depend on. We evaluate six multimodal LLMs (8B to 72B+, open and closed) under a fixed zero-shot protocol. Five QA categories, 39.3% of our stratified set, return zero correct answers from every model; on the arithmetic task, every model that emits a number does worse than a constant-mean baseline, with exact- and near-match scores coinciding: decorrelation, not approximation. Auditing our own metrics surfaced two artifacts in opposite directions: we correct a refusal-scoring bug and report the fixed scores beside the originals, and flag an inflated metadata metric as an upper bound. KhatianDoc documents not a performance gap but the absence of a capability, with verified ground truth for future systems. Code and data, with a redacted image release, are publicly available.

</details>


### [151] [Remember and Reweight: Enhancing Multi-Agent Debate with Experience Memory and Confidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03619)
*Xuanfa Jin,Zhijian Ma,Yongcheng Zeng,Xinyu Cui,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 R²-MAD，通过历史辩论经验记忆、状态感知检索与可靠性重加权，缓解多智能体辩论中的共享误解问题，并在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论能够提升大语言模型推理能力，但当多数智能体一开始形成错误共识时，辩论会放大错误而非纠正错误。现有方法主要处理同伴影响偏斜，未充分解决智能体自身概念先验偏差。

Method: 提出 Remember and Reweight for Multi-Agent Debate（R²-MAD）。该框架为智能体引入从历史辩论中积累的经验记忆；使用辩论状态感知的检索策略，根据当前共识程度动态检索相关历史证据以校准概念先验；再基于检索经验估计各智能体可靠性，生成置信权重以调节同伴影响。

Result: 在多种基准任务上的实验显示，R²-MAD 相较单智能体方法和现有 MAD 基线均取得稳定性能提升。

Conclusion: R²-MAD 通过“记忆检索”和“可靠性重加权”同时干预共享误解与同伴影响问题，为提升多智能体辩论鲁棒性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate (MAD) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models by having multiple agents iteratively refine their responses through discussion. However, MAD suffers from a critical vulnerability known as shared misconception: when a majority of agents initially converge on an incorrect answer, the debate process tends to amplify rather than correct the error. Existing methods primarily address peer skew but leave the agents' inherently biased concept priors unaddressed. To mitigate this systematic weakness, we propose R$^2$-MAD (Remember and Reweight for Multi-Agent Debate), a framework that equips agents with an experience memory accumulated from past debates. R$^2$-MAD intervenes on both failure modes through two complementary mechanisms: A debate-state-aware retrieval policy dynamically calibrates the concept prior by retrieving relevant historical evidence based on the current consensus level. Then these retrieved experiences provide a basis for estimating per-agent reliability, yielding confidence weights to modulate peer influence. Experiments on various benchmarks show that R$^2$-MAD achieves consistent improvements over existing single-agent and MAD baselines.

</details>


### [152] [</think> Doesn't Stop Reasoning: Analysis of Spurious CoT Termination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03633)
*Seunghee Koh,Sungjae Choi,Minchan Kwon,Sunghyun Baek,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究训练-free早退方法中注入结束思考标记（EoT）并不总能真正终止推理，提出并验证“伪CoT终止”现象及其与模型对注入EoT注意不足的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT可提升大型推理模型在复杂任务上的表现，但会产生冗长、冗余的推理轨迹。早退方法通过在中间位置停止推理来节省token，但作者发现仅注入EoT标记未必能让模型干净地从推理阶段切换到回答阶段，因此需要系统分析这一控制失效现象。

Method: 作者考察一种在早退点注入EoT（</think>）以触发回答阶段的策略，定义并测量回答阶段中在模型重新生成EoT之前仍表现出推理行为的片段，即“伪CoT终止”。他们进一步提出/使用Exit-token Attention Biasing（EAB）增强模型对注入EoT的注意力，并在四个大型推理模型、五个基准任务和两种早退方法上进行验证。

Result: 实验发现，注入EoT后模型的回答阶段可能继续生成推理式内容，并且重新生成EoT之前的长度会随早退节省的推理token数量增加而增长。提高模型对注入EoT的注意力后，伪CoT终止现象减少，回答阶段长度也缩短。

Conclusion: 论文表明，外部插入EoT以匹配显式think-block格式并不足以保证大型推理模型真正完成从推理到回答的状态转换；模型控制不能仅依赖格式级token插入，还需考虑模型内部对控制token的利用与注意机制。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning improves large reasoning models (LRMs) on complex tasks but often produces long, redundant traces. Recent training-free early-exit methods shorten these traces by choosing an intermediate point to stop reasoning. We study one such strategy that injects an end-of-think token (EoT, </think>) at this point to trigger the reasoning-to-answering transition, and find that the injected EoT does not always induce a clean answering phase. Answering-phase generation can continue before the model regenerates another EoT, with the span preceding this regenerated EoT scaling with the reasoning tokens saved by early exit and exhibiting continued reasoning behavior. We call this spurious CoT termination, where reasoning-like generation continues into the answering phase. We hypothesize that insufficient attention to the injected EoT contributes to spurious CoT termination and probe this hypothesis with Exit-token Attention Biasing (EAB). Across four LRMs, five benchmarks, and two early-exit methods, increasing attention to the injected EoT reduces spurious CoT termination and answering-phase length. These results reveal a limitation of controlling LRMs by externally matching their explicit think-block format. Inserting the EoT token conforms to this format but does not by itself guarantee the intended reasoning-to-answering transition. Our code is available at https://github.com/Seunghee-Koh/Spurious-CoT-Termination.

</details>


### [153] [The Impact of Synthetic Data Augmentation on Discourse-Pragmatic Function Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03652)
*Sara Sorahi,Kevin Tang,Reza Kazemian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究合成数据在表示空间中相对真实样本的位置对低资源语用功能分类的影响，发现靠近真实数据核心区域的合成样本最能提升宏平均F值，而距离均衡混合样本带来最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP类别不均衡处理中的合成数据增强多关注生成样本数量与多样性，较少考察合成样本与真实训练数据在嵌入空间中的几何关系；而语篇语用功能分类天然存在数据稀疏问题，因此需要理解“合成样本落在何处”是否影响分类效果。

Method: 作者使用410个手工标注的英语词look实例，覆盖Attention Signal、Directive、Discourse Marker和Interjection四类功能；用Llama 3.1生成合成训练样本，并在RoBERTa嵌入空间中按其与真实训练数据的余弦距离进行划分；在保持增强数量一致的前提下，比较六种合成样本相对经验决策边界位置不同的训练条件。

Result: 所有增强条件相较仅使用真实数据的基线都提升了宏平均F值和准确率；其中靠近真实样本核心区域的NEAR条件带来最大宏平均F值提升（+0.113），距离均衡混合策略取得最高准确率（0.748）；但所有条件均未改善AUC。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强的效果不仅取决于生成数量，也显著取决于合成样本在表示空间中的几何位置。增强主要改变分类决策边界，而非提升模型概率估计质量；该发现对低资源语用分类任务中的合成数据选择与过滤具有启示意义。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation has become a common strategy for addressing class imbalance in NLP, but most approaches focus on the quantity and diversity of generated examples rather than their geometric relationship to real training data. We investigate this question in the context of discourse pragmatic function classification, a task where data sparsity is a structural feature rather than a collection artefact. Using 410 manually annotated instances of the English word look drawn from the British National Corpus, spanning four functions: Attention Signal, Directive, Discourse Marker, and Interjection. We generate synthetic training examples with Llama 3.1 and partition them by their cosine distance from real training data in RoBERTa embedding space. We compare six training conditions that differ in the placement of synthetic examples relative to the empirical decision boundary, while holding augmentation quantity constant across conditions. All augmented conditions improve macro F and accuracy over the real only baseline, but core proximal examples (NEAR) yield the largest gains in macro F (0.113), while a distance balanced mix achieves the highest accuracy (0.748). No condition improves AUC, indicating that augmentation shifts the decision boundary rather than improving the model's underlying probability estimates. These findings suggest that where synthetic examples land in representation space matters as much as how many are generated, with implications for low resource pragmatic classification more broadly.

</details>


### [154] [Enhancing Financial Question Answering: A Novel Benchmark Dataset of Banks' financial statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03654)
*Arianna Miola,Bruno Spaccavento,Lorenzo Silotto,Marco Bianchetti,Luca Cagliero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinRAG-QA提出了一个面向银行年报与Pillar 3报告的跨机构金融问答基准，并系统评估多阶段RAG流水线，发现检索增强和生成模型选择对性能影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行财务报表篇幅长、术语密集、文本与数值信息混杂，且不同司法辖区和机构之间披露口径不均一，使自动问答尤其是跨机构比较分析面临困难；现有金融QA基准多聚焦美国单一机构文件，难以覆盖真实从业场景。

Method: 构建FinRAG-QA数据集：基于24家欧美主要银行2019—2023年的209份年度报告和Pillar 3报告，由从业者围绕10个标准化指标设计999个问题。实验评估多阶段RAG流程，并分别分析上下文块增强、检索优化嵌入模型、交叉编码器重排序、生成模型和上下文规模等组件的贡献。

Result: 上下文块增强结合检索优化嵌入模型将NDCG@10从0.322提升至0.710。在真实答案证据已被检索到的条件下，推理优化生成器将答案准确率从44.6%提升至79.0%，但生成延迟约增加20倍。实验还发现，当首阶段排序已较强时，交叉编码器重排序反而损害检索效果；生成阶段使用单个最高排名块优于更大上下文。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了更贴近真实跨银行比较分析场景的金融QA基准，并表明高质量检索、块级上下文增强与合适的生成模型是提升金融RAG问答性能的关键；同时，常见的重排序和扩大上下文策略并非总是有效，需要根据任务和检索质量审慎配置。

Abstract: The comparative analysis of banks' financial statements poses significant challenges for automated question answering systems due to their complexity, substantial length, technical language, and inhomogeneity of both textual and numerical content across different jurisdictions and institutions. We introduce FinRAG-QA, a novel benchmark dataset for financial question answering, which comprises 999 practitioner-curated questions on 10 standardised indicators, grounded in 209 annual and Pillar 3 reports from 24 major European and U.S. banks spanning 2019-2023. Unlike prior financial QA benchmarks, which centre on U.S. filings and single-institution analysis, FinRAG-QA targets cross-institutional retrieval over documents averaging 198k words, longer than any existing financial QA resource. On this benchmark we evaluate a multi-stage RAG pipeline and isolate the contribution of each component. Contextual chunk enrichment combined with a retrieval-optimised embedding model raises NDCG@10 from 0.322 to 0.710; conditional on the ground truth being retrieved, a reasoning-optimised generator raises answer accuracy from 44.6% to 79.0% (+34.4 percentage points), at roughly 20x the generation latency. We further show that cross-encoder reranking degrades retrieval when the first-stage ranking is already strong, and that a single top-ranked chunk outperforms larger contexts at generation time. Experiments were run in late 2024-early 2025 with the models available at that time.

</details>


### [155] [A Circuit for Plural Reference: How LLMs Represent and Retrieve Singular and Plural Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03687)
*Anh Danh,Rick Nouwen,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在复数指代中如何表示、检索单数与复数实体，并识别出若干关键注意力头参与共指信息表示、复数实体组合识别和代词预测信息传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共指消解是语境推理中的关键任务，但大语言模型如何在内部机制上处理复数指代、如何将多个先前实体组合为复数先行词并预测相应代词，仍缺乏清晰解释。

Method: 作者结合机制可解释性与注意力模式分析，考察模型预测代词回指先前实体的过程；并使用多种因果干预技术定位在输入表征、复数实体识别、信息传递与先行词选择中发挥作用的注意力头。

Result: 研究发现一组注意力头分别负责表示输入中的共指信息、识别构成复数指代的实体，以及将相关信息传递给负责选择先行词和预测代词的组件。同时，模型在复数代词偏好上与人类表现一致：本体上相似且由“and”连接的实体更可能被视为复数实体。

Conclusion: 论文表明，大语言模型的复数共指处理并非完全黑箱，而是可由特定注意力头和信息流机制解释；其复数代词选择还反映出类似人类的语义与句法偏好。

Abstract: Coreference resolution is an important task in contextual reasoning. In this paper, we investigate the mechanism for representing and retrieving singular and plural entities for plural reference. We use a combination of mechanistic interpretability and attention pattern analysis to study the process in which LLMs predict a pronoun to refer back to previously mentioned entities. Using a range of causal intervention techniques, we find a set of attention heads that are responsible for (1) representing coreference information in the input, (2) identifying entities that form a plural reference, (3) transferring the information to the component that is responsible for selecting the antecedents and predicting the pronoun. We also find that LLMs align with humans in preference for plural pronoun. Specifically, entities in a plural construction are more likely to be referred to as a plural entity if they are ontologically similar and are linked by the conjunction "and".

</details>


### [156] [Opening mind by opening architecture: analysis strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03719)
*Francesco Vitucci,Giuseppe Silvi,Daniele Giuseppe Annese,Francesco Scagliola,Anthony Di Furia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文关注电声作曲中的数字信号处理研究环境流失问题，主张通过历史性案例与分析工具重建对音频处理器内部机制的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着商业化数字音频工具广泛使用，闭源/封闭架构音频处理器占据主导，用户虽能描述输出的感知特征，却难以理解其内部过程、实现逻辑及交互系统。这导致技术史、审美后果与研究方法意识的缺失。

Method: 文章以 Manfred Schroeder 的历史混响器为案例，结合文献回溯与技术分析，构建用于评价其信号处理结构与声学结果的分析工具，并进行实践性实现，以形成可研究、可复现、可理解的学习路径。

Result: 研究展示了如何从历史算法出发，建立分析评价与实际实现之间的联系，使研究者能够重新进入被商业黑箱遮蔽的处理环境，理解算法实现过程及其审美影响。

Conclusion: 数字信号处理技术不仅是工具，也承载实现历史与美学意义。通过文献、案例复现和科学分析方法，可以恢复对音频处理器内部机制的批判性认识，并促进更自觉的电声创作与研究。

Abstract: In numerical signal processing for electroacoustic composition, the progressive loss of specific development and research environments caused by the increasing use of digital market tools has favoured the dominance of the closed-architecture audio processor model. This model, while powerful, envisions the possibility of describing output data about its perceived characteristics, but at the cost of ignoring its internal process and interacting systems, which become complex, powerful environments but closed in an inscrutable black box, a loss we must consider. Any digital signal processing technique tells a story. Just as the words of a language incorporate social, historical and technical polysemic layers, a signal processor has its own story of implementation, a gradual technological achievement with its inevitable aesthetic consequences. Through the looking-glass of literature, one can access those environments with renewed awareness by reestablishing a scientific method and an attitude to research. In this specific case, starting from the case study of Manfred Schroeder's historical reverbs, we illustrate the process of building analytical evaluation tools, as well as practical implementation, at the basis of a conscious study path.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond BLEU: A Case for Redefining Sign Language Translation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03734)
*Oline Ranum,Edward Fish,Simon Hadfield,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出 BLEU-4 不能充分衡量手语翻译模型的真实手语理解能力，并提出一种基于开放权重大语言模型问答协议的内容保真评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语翻译评估高度依赖 BLEU-4，但该指标源自口语/书面语机器翻译，可能无法反映手语的空间—时间理解与内容传递质量；在低资源、多模态场景中，模型还可能利用伪相关和目标语言先验获得高分。

Method: 作者在 Phoenix-2014T 和 CSL-Daily 两个数据集上评估六种手语翻译模型，分析空间—时间理解能力与 BLEU-4 的关系；同时提出受语言学习评估启发的开放权重 LLM 问答协议，用于衡量显著内容是否被保留，并与人工排序、释义不变性和训练测试重叠鲁棒性进行比较。

Result: 实验显示 BLEU-4 的提升并不必然代表更好的手语理解。新 QA 协议与人工排序更一致，且相较 BLEU-4 对释义的鲁棒性高 6 到 7 倍；在 Phoenix-2014T 上，五个无 gloss 系统表现大多处于噪声范围内，而 gloss 监督系统高出 9.3 分，这一差距在 BLEU-4 下不可见。

Conclusion: 论文认为 SLT 评估应从表层文本重合转向内容传递与手语理解能力评估；所提出的 LLM-QA 协议能更准确揭示模型差异，并提示当前 BLEU-4 主导的评估可能误导领域进展判断。

Abstract: BLEU-4 is the standard metric for evaluating sign language translation (SLT), but spoken-language metrics may not adequately reflect sign language proficiency. The multimodal, low-resource context of SLT allows models to exploit spurious correlations and spoken-language priors, rather than learning stronger sign representations. In this paper, we evaluate the relationship between spatio-temporal understanding and BLEU-4 across six SLT models on Phoenix-2014T and CSL-Daily, showing that gains in BLEU-4 are not on their own evidence of better sign language understanding. This work introduces an alternative inspired by language-learning assessment, using an open-weight-LLM QA protocol that measures salient content preservation. It aligns more closely with human rankings and is six to seven times more paraphrase-invariant than BLEU-4. Applied to SLT, this protocol targets content transfer, is more robust to train-test overlap, and gives a different picture of the field: the five gloss-free systems are largely within noise of one another on Phoenix-2014T, while the gloss-supervised system stands 9.3 points higher, a gap invisible to BLEU-4.

</details>


### [158] [Typological Feature Prediction with Large Language Models: An In-Context Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03775)
*Qianwen Wang,York Hay Ng,Aditya Khan,En-Shiun Annie Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型通过上下文学习预测缺失的语言类型学特征，并发现加入谱系与地理邻近证据后效果显著优于基线，同时可提供较一致的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言类型学特征在多语言自然语言处理中应用广泛，预测缺失特征具有下游价值；但现有方法通常缺乏可解释的预测依据，且在不同资源水平语言与不同特征类型上的表现尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者使用 URIEL+ 与 Glottolog 的语言学数据，设计基于上下文学习的提示方法，让大语言模型在预测类型学特征时利用目标语言的谱系邻居和地理邻居证据，并评估其预测性能与生成理由的一致性。

Result: 零样本提示效果不足；在提供谱系和地理邻近证据后，大语言模型显著超过所有基线方法，并且没有对低资源语言产生明显不利影响。多数模型生成的理由与所提供证据一致。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在有证据支持的上下文学习设置下，可有效进行可解释的语言类型学特征预测，为解释型类型学预测方法提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Typological features are widely used in multilingual NLP, and the prediction of such features holds downstream utility. However, existing methods to predict missing values lack interpretable justifications for predictions, while their performance across resource levels and feature types remains underexplored. Given LLMs' abilities in meta-linguistic reasoning and in providing rationales, we investigate LLMs' performance in typological feature prediction via an in-context learning approach with linguistic data from URIEL+ and Glottolog. We find that zero-shot prompting is insufficient, but when given phylogenetic and geographic neighbour evidence, LLMs substantially outperform all baselines without disadvantaging low-resource languages. We further find that most LLM rationales are consistent with the provided evidence, offering a step toward explainable typological feature prediction.

</details>


### [159] [IndicSafeEval: Safety Robustness of Large Language Models under Multilingual Persuasive Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03781)
*Saikat Mondal,Mamta,Deeksha Varshney,Oana Cocarascu,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IndicSafeEval，一个面向印度多语言场景的说服式越狱安全评测框架，用于评估开源大语言模型在不同语言、说服策略与风险类别下的安全一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型安全评测主要集中于英语，难以揭示低资源及文化多样语言中的对齐失效问题；现实用户请求也可能以不同语言和说服性表达出现，因此需要多语言、说服感知的安全基准。

Method: 构建包含10类安全关键内容、6种类人说服策略、4种印度语言（Hindi、Bengali、Marathi、Punjabi）的基准，共7,200条对抗提示；对多个开源LLM进行系统性黑盒评测，比较语言、提示风格和风险类别对安全行为的影响。

Result: 实验显示模型安全表现并非跨语言和跨提示风格一致；安全性能显著受所用语言与说服性措辞影响。不同风险类别的脆弱性也存在差异，部分有害内容更容易被说服式越狱诱导。

Conclusion: 当前以英语为中心的安全评测存在重要局限；需要多语言且能覆盖说服式攻击的评测框架，以更准确衡量真实场景中的LLM安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multilingual settings, yet their safety is still evaluated primarily in English. This limits our understanding of how alignment failures manifest in low-resource and culturally diverse languages. We introduce IndicSafeEval, a persuasion-based jailbreak evaluation framework for Indian languages. Our benchmark combines ten safety critical content categories with six human-like persuasive strategies across four different Indian languages, such as Hindi, Bengali, Marathi and Punjabi, resulting in 7,200 adversarial prompts. We conduct a systematic black-box evaluation of several open-source LLMs to examine how their safety behaviour varies across languages, persuasion strategies, and risk categories. Our analysis shows that the model does not behave equally safely across all languages and prompt styles. Instead, safety performance depends strongly on both the languages used and the way a request is phrased using persuasive cues. We further observe that different risk categories exhibit different levels of vulnerability, with some types of harmful content being significantly more susceptible to persuasion-based jailbreaks than others. These findings reveal important limitations of current safety evaluations, which are largely English-centric, and underscore the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware benchmarking frameworks to more accurately assess real-world LLM safety. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [160] [Evaluating Criterion-Conditioned Behaviour of Large Language Models in Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03814)
*Danting Zhang,Bei Peng,Robert Loftin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DECO 与成对评估方法，用于检验大语言模型是否能按具体审核准则进行内容判断；结果显示，传统聚合标签上的高分会掩盖模型在准则层面的显著失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核基准通常把多个审核准则合并为单一标签，难以判断模型是否真正理解并区分不同准则，也无法验证其能否在给定具体准则时稳定地作出正确决策。

Method: 作者提出 Diagnostic Evaluation of COntent（DECO），将内容因素与审核准则解耦，支持受控的准则级评估；同时引入成对评估，在同一输入下比较模型针对不同准则的输出差异。实验覆盖四个内容审核数据集和四个大语言模型。

Result: 实验发现，即使模型在标准基准上表现较强，仍可能在具体审核准则层面出现严重失败。模型尤其难以处理那些不能仅凭整体有害性判断、而必须依据特定内容维度或准则要求进行判断的案例。

Conclusion: 聚合标签上的高性能不足以证明大语言模型能够可靠地执行细粒度内容审核。未来评估方法应显式衡量模型的准则条件化行为，即模型是否能根据不同审核标准对同一内容作出相应、可区分且一致的判断。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong performance on standard content moderation benchmarks. However, these benchmarks often aggregate multiple moderation criteria into a single label, making it unclear whether models can disentangle them and reliably apply each criterion when making decisions. To study whether LLMs exhibit criterion-conditioned behaviour, we introduce Diagnostic Evaluation of COntent (DECO), a criterion-independent factorisation of content that enables controlled, criterion-level evaluation. We also introduce pairwise evaluation to compare model outputs across different criteria for the same input. Across four moderation datasets and four LLMs, we find that strong benchmark performance can hide substantial failures at the criterion level. Models struggle most when correct decisions depend not on overall harmfulness, but on the specific aspect of the content that the criterion requires them to assess. Our results highlight a key limitation of current content moderation benchmarks: strong performance on aggregated labels does not provide sufficient evidence that LLMs can reliably evaluate content with respect to individual moderation criteria. These findings call for the development of evaluation methods that explicitly measure criterion-conditioned behaviour.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Shallow Alignment: How Post-Training Methods Determine Refusal Circuits And Steering Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03887)
*Hoang Cuong Nguyen,Mark Dras,Usman Naseem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较三种语言模型拒绝有害请求的后训练方法，发现训练方法会显著改变模型内部的拒绝计算方式，但现有方法都无法同时满足稳健、安全与可编辑的理想对齐目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在回答：用于训练语言模型拒绝不当请求的不同后训练方法，如何影响模型内部实际执行拒绝行为的机制；并评估这些方法在可靠安全对齐方面是否足够稳健。

Method: 作者比较了监督微调、带推理链的微调以及偏好优化（ORPO）三种后训练方法，并在 Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B、Qwen3-8B 三种架构不同的模型上进行分析，考察拒绝行为的内部表示、组件集中程度、可操控性以及对通用能力的影响。

Result: 结果表明，训练方法而不仅是数据，会重塑模型内部的拒绝计算；带推理链的训练在三种模型中都形成了可区分的拒绝计算模式。同时，模型架构也会独立影响内部结构以及拒绝行为被定向调控的可靠性。

Conclusion: 当前研究中的后训练方法都未能同时实现三项理想属性：拒绝机制不依赖少数脆弱组件、提升安全性且不损害通用能力、以及可通过小规模定向编辑进行纠正。因此，现有后训练对齐方法不应被视为已解决且可靠的防护方案，尤其不适合在高风险场景中盲目依赖。

Abstract: How do the methods used to train language models to refuse harmful requests shape how that refusal actually works inside the model? We compare three post-training methods - supervised fine-tuning, reasoning-augmented fine-tuning (training on reasoning chains that justify a safety decision), and preference optimization (ORPO) - across three architecturally distinct models (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B, Qwen3-8B). We find that training method, not just data, reshapes how refusal is computed internally: reasoning-augmented training consistently produces a distinct kind of refusal computation, visible across all three models, while architecture independently shapes internal structure and how reliably refusal can be steered. Most importantly, no method we study achieves all three properties we would want from safe alignment at once: refusal that isn't concentrated in a few fragile components, safety gains that don't cost general capability, and safety behavior correctable through small, targeted edits. We caution against treating current post-training methods as a solved, reliable defense, especially for security-critical use. Code and models are available in https://github.com/hoangcuongnguyen2001/Beyond-Shallow-Alignment.

</details>


### [162] [CROCODIL: Cross-Model Code Editing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03894)
*Linghan Zhong,Aditya Thimmaiah,Jayanth Srinivasa,Milos Gligoric,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究不同大语言模型在编辑彼此生成的“外来代码”时的行为差异，发现模型往往会进行更多甚至过度的修改，并提出 CROCODIL 后训练框架以减少不必要改动同时保持功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发团队常混合使用多个 LLM 编程助手，不同模型因训练数据与代码风格偏好不同，可能需要编辑由其他模型生成的代码。作者关注这种跨模型代码编辑场景下是否会引发额外或过度修改。

Method: 作者提出 CROCODIL（Cross-model Code Editing with LLMs），一种后训练框架。其奖励由两部分构成：相似性奖励用于惩罚过大的代码变更，执行奖励用于衡量构建与测试是否成功；二者相乘以鼓励模型在不降低任务成功率的前提下减小编辑规模。

Result: 实验发现，LLM 在编辑外来代码时倾向于做更多修改，且这些修改常常是过度的。CROCODIL 能够减少编辑幅度，同时维持代码编辑任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 跨模型代码编辑会受到模型风格差异影响，导致不必要的大规模修改；通过结合相似性与执行正确性的后训练奖励机制，可以缓解过度编辑问题并提升多模型协作场景下的代码维护稳定性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous tools for code generation and editing. However, development teams often use multiple LLM assistants. Different developers may prefer different models, and individual developers may switch between models across different coding sessions. Because of this, the edits any one model makes are frequently applied to foreign code originally generated by another model. These LLMs are often trained on different datasets, and as a result have different stylistic preferences. Do LLMs behave differently when they edit foreign code originally written by a different LLM with a different coding style? We find that models tend to make more, and often excessive, edits on foreign code. We introduce CROCODIL (Cross-model Code Editing with LLMs), a post-training framework for reducing excessive edits while preserving functional correctness. CROCODIL's similarity reward penalizes large changes, while its execution reward scores build and test success. We use the product of these two rewards to encourage the policy to decrease the edit size without decreasing the edit task success rate. CROCODIL is available at https://github.com/EngineeringSoftware/Crocodil.

</details>


### [163] [RuleMem: Active Rule Memory for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03915)
*Xingyuan Zeng,Zuohan Wu,Quanming Yao,Yue Wang,Wei Liu,Libin Zheng,Jiuke Wang,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RuleMem 将长期对话历史归纳为可复用的自然语言 Horn 规则，并用规则困惑度一致性机制验证规则，从而同时改进证据检索与答案推理，在长期对话问答基准上显著优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话问答需要跨越大量、时间分散的对话历史进行推理；现有记忆机制多把历史信息作为被动事实存储，容易产生语义鸿沟，导致检索不到语义距离较远但相关的证据，并削弱推理可靠性。

Method: 提出 RuleMem：从历史交互中构建自然语言 Horn clauses，归纳可复用逻辑规则；通过 Rule Perplexity Consistency（RPC）机制验证规则一致性与可靠性；利用规则主动指导证据检索，并为答案生成提供显式逻辑结构。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval_s* 两个长期对话基准上进行评估；在 LoCoMo 上与 14 个基线严格比较，RuleMem 取得最高准确率，比基线平均值高 27.47 个百分点，相对提升 54.3%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将历史对话从被动记忆转化为主动可推理的规则记忆，能够缓解语义检索鸿沟并提升长期对话问答的可靠性与准确性。

Abstract: Question answering agents in long-term conversations must reason over massive, temporally dispersed dialogue histories. However, existing memory mechanisms primarily treat past information as \textit{passively} stored facts, leading to semantic gaps and unreliable reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose RuleMem, a rule-based memory framework that induces reusable logical rules from historical interactions to \textit{actively} guide both evidence retrieval and reasoning. Specifically, RuleMem constructs natural-language Horn clauses from conversations and validates them via a Rule Perplexity Consistency (RPC) mechanism. These induced rules enable the retrieval of semantically distant evidence while providing an explicit logical structure for answer generation. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation of RuleMem on two long-term conversational benchmarks, LoCoMo and LongMemEval_s*. In a rigorous comparison against 14 baselines on LoCoMo, RuleMem achieved the highest accuracy, exceeding the baseline average by 27.47 points (a 54.3% relative improvement).

</details>


### [164] [Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio: Quantifying the Dual-Track Mechanism of Gender Assignment in Latvian Loanwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03930)
*Yelingyun Zhang,Atis Kapenieks,Marina Platonova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出固定后缀依赖比（FSDR），用于区分外来词性别分配中由固定形态后缀驱动的“形态锚定”与较自由的默认性别选择，并发现拉脱维亚语英语借词中阳性更集中于自由选择区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多次发现英语借词在不同接受语言中倾向于聚集到阳性，但该模式的来源不清楚，因为固定形态规则与默认性别分配常被混合分析，难以判断阳性聚集究竟来自形态后缀约束还是默认机制。

Method: 研究提出Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio（FSDR）来量化不同性别对固定派生后缀的依赖程度，并分析1,832个拉脱维亚语名词词元类型，以比较阳性与阴性借词在固定后缀区和自由选择区中的分布差异，同时考察该模式是否具有借词特异性及其当代变化。

Result: 结果显示借词系统内部存在显著的FSDR不对称：阴性借词更依赖固定派生后缀，阳性借词则更集中于自由选择区；该分布模式具有借词特异性，并且在当代使用中更加明显。

Conclusion: FSDR为检验默认性别分配提供了量化框架，表明在语言接触情境下，阳性默认机制可能被激活并进一步强化，从而解释英语借词阳性聚集现象的一部分来源。

Abstract: Existing research has repeatedly observed the tendency for English loanwords to cluster in the masculine gender across different recipient languages, yet the origin of this pattern remains difficult to determine, as fixed morphological rules and default assignments are frequently analysed together. This study proposes the Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio (FSDR) to quantify the degree of reliance on fixed derivational suffixes across different genders, and to distinguish between morphological anchoring and free-choice in distribution. By examining 1,832 Latvian noun lemma types, the results reveal a significant FSDR asymmetry within the loanword system: feminine loanwords rely significantly more on fixed derivational suffixes, while masculine loanwords are more concentrated in the free-choice zone. This pattern exhibits loanword specificity and has become more pronounced in contemporary usage. FSDR therefore provides a quantitative framework for testing default gender and shows how masculine default can be activated and reinforced under language contact.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Majority Vote: Multi-Perspective Adjudication for Medical Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03953)
*Joe Cecil,Marjorie Freedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出长文本聊天机器人回答中的事实错误常被单次标注低估；通过多视角、多轮裁决可提升错误发现覆盖率，但结果仍依赖专家判断、证据质量与裁决标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估聊天机器人生成文本中的事实错误频率及检测系统可靠性，对安全性评估至关重要；但现有事实错误检测常采用单轮、单标注者流程，容易遗漏长文本中隐蔽且夹杂在正确内容中的错误。

Method: 研究构建了针对医学相关聊天机器人回答的多视角标注流程，结合首轮人工标注、LLM-as-a-Judge候选错误发现，以及两类裁决方式：医学专家裁决和基于证据的事实核查；同时将该技术应用于已有基准进行验证。

Result: 首轮标注者经常遗漏后来被裁决者确认的事实错误；LLM-as-a-Judge能提升候选错误发现率，但单独使用仍会漏掉人工标注者发现的错误；不同裁决者之间也存在分歧；在既有基准上同样发现漏标模式。

Conclusion: 单轮幻觉/事实错误基准虽然便于扩大规模，但可能系统性低估事实错误；多轮、多来源候选加裁决可提升覆盖率，但基准结论仍受判断标准、专业知识和证据依据影响。

Abstract: Understanding the frequency of factual errors in chatbot-generated text and evaluating systems that detect these errors is critical for determining chatbot safety. Yet factual-error detection is often treated as a single-pass, single-annotator labeling problem. In long-form chatbot responses, factual errors can be subtle and embedded within mostly correct text.
  We develop a multi-perspective annotation study of medically relevant chatbot responses, combining first-pass annotation, LLM-as-a-Judge (LaJ) candidate discovery, and two forms of adjudication: medical-expert and evidence-based fact-checking. First-pass annotators frequently miss factual errors later validated by adjudicators. LaJ improves candidate discovery, but is insufficient on its own: It misses factual errors that annotators catch. We also find disagreement among adjudicators, suggesting that adjudication over multiple candidate sources can improve benchmark completeness, but does not eliminate the need to apply judgment and expertise. Applied to an existing benchmark, this technique reveals a similar pattern of missing annotations. Together, these results suggest that in the settings examined here, single-pass hallucination benchmarks may achieve scale at the cost of undercounting factual errors. Multi-pass adjudication can improve coverage, but inferences drawn from the benchmarks are still sensitive to the judgment, expertise, and evidence used to determine error presence.

</details>


### [166] [Two-Stage Reinforcement Learning for Sound and Adversarial Test Generation in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03955)
*Jiacheng Xu,Wentao Zhang,Zhiyi Lyu,Fuxiang Zhang,Chaojie Wang,Yang Liu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Test Cases Scaling（TCS），通过两阶段强化学习训练测试用例生成器，为代码生成模型生成高质量、具区分性的测试用例，从而提升代码生成与答案选择效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成中的强化学习依赖可执行反馈，而反馈主要来自测试用例；但高质量测试用例需要同时满足正确性（soundness）和区分性（discriminativeness），现实中较为稀缺。因此，作者希望利用已学习模型自动生成有效测试用例，尤其是能暴露当前求解器错误的反例测试。

Method: 作者将测试用例生成建模为一种对抗式强化学习问题，并提出两阶段框架 TCS。两个阶段都从滚动的、与当前策略对齐的缓冲区训练测试生成器：第一阶段生成与参考解一致的合法测试；第二阶段将训练缓冲区限制在当前模型失败模式上，使生成器学习产生针对性反例测试。

Result: 在 TACO 和 LiveCodeBench 上，TCS 提升了代码生成模型的 pass@1 表现，并改善了推理阶段基于生成测试进行答案选择的效果。此外，训练得到的测试生成器还能有效用于筛选其他大语言模型产生的候选答案。

Conclusion: TCS 表明，自动生成测试用例可以作为代码生成强化学习中的可扩展反馈来源；通过针对当前失败模式生成反例测试，模型能够获得更强的训练信号和更可靠的推理时选择能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has substantially advanced code generation with large language models (LLMs) through executable feedback. The feedback for coding problems mainly comes from specific test cases, where high-quality test cases are often scarce since they should be both sound and discriminative. We thus turn to study the auto-generation of test cases using the learned model. We find this is naturally an adversarial RL problem: the model is expected to generate effective test cases as counterexamples, depending on the solver's current failure modes. We propose Test Cases Scaling (TCS), a two-stage RL framework for effective test generation. Both stages train a test generator from a rolling policy-aligned buffer: Stage 1 generates tests consistent with the reference solution, and Stage 2 restricts the buffer to current failure modes and learns counterexample tests. Across TACO and LiveCodeBench, TCS improves both pass@1 and inference-time answer selection according to generated tests. We find the learned test generator also enables effective selection among other LLM outputs.

</details>


### [167] [Investigating the Ability of Large Language Models to Analyze Recipes for Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03967)
*Revathy Venkataramanan,Aditya Luthra,Venkatesan Nadimuthu,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型判断给定食谱是否适合糖尿病饮食的能力，并构建了包含7607个食谱的基准数据集。结果显示，能够结合饮食指南进行推理的模型表现更好，Mistral-7B和Llama 70B整体最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LLM可用于膳食规划，但针对特定健康需求（如糖尿病）评估食谱适宜性仍具挑战。模型需要检索并理解相关饮食指南，解析食谱成分与烹饪方式，并据此进行可靠判断。

Method: 作者设计三类提示方式：直接查询提示、上下文引导提示、示例上下文提示，分别向模型提供不同程度的糖尿病饮食指南信息；同时构建包含7607个食谱的评测数据集，其中3807个适合糖尿病，3800个不适合糖尿病，用于比较不同LLM的分类表现。

Result: 多数LLM在预测食谱适合糖尿病时较为保守，以避免潜在不良后果；具备基于饮食指南进行推理能力的模型表现更优。Mistral-7B和Llama 70B相较其他模型表现突出。

Conclusion: LLM具备一定的糖尿病食谱适宜性分析能力，但性能依赖于提示中指南信息的质量以及模型推理能力。结合医学饮食指南的提示策略能提升模型判断准确性。

Abstract: Several studies have evaluated the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) for meal planning, yielding positive outcomes. These models can process natural language inputs and leverage learned knowledge from their pretraining to generate meal plans. In this work, we investigate the ability of LLMs to analyze the suitability of given recipes for diabetes. The primary challenge for LLMs is to retrieve relevant dietary guidelines for diabetes, decompose recipes into ingredients and cooking methods, and apply these guidelines to determine the recipe's suitability. To study these challenges, we employ three kinds of prompts namely, (i) Direct Query Prompt (ii) Context-Guided Prompt, and (iii) Exemplary Context Prompt that incorporate different levels of diabetes dietary guidelines from medical sources. We introduce a benchmark dataset curated for this investigation consisting of 7607 recipes that include 3807 recipes suitable for diabetes and 3800 recipes not suitable for diabetes. Our results demonstrate that most LLMs are cautious in predicting recipes as suitable to prevent detrimental outcomes. Further, the models that can reason using the dietary guidelines performed better in predicting the suitability of recipes for diabetes. Overall, Mistral-7B and Llama 70B showed superior performance to their counterparts.

</details>


### [168] [Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox for Voice Dubbing and Full-Duplex Dialogue Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03992)
*Sanyuan Chen,Min-Jae Hwang,Sho Inoue,Anna Sun,Bokai Yu,David Kant,Dongmin Hyun,Dorian Desblancs,Gregory Antonovsky,Oleg Repin,Peng-Jen Chen,Xutai Ma,Zehai Tu,Juan Pino,Wei-Ning Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Text-AB 是一个基于潜在扩散与流匹配训练的统一文本到语音框架，可用于高质量配音和全双工对话合成；其核心特点是高压缩高保真的 DAC-VAE 表征、无需显式对齐的文本条件建模，以及大规模预训练与多任务微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音配音与对话合成系统通常依赖强制对齐、显式时长预测或低压缩率音频表示，导致训练流程复杂、长语音生成效率受限，并且在韵律、说话人相似度、自然度、轮次交互和情感表达方面仍有提升空间。论文旨在构建一个统一、可扩展、对齐自由且适用于多种语音生成任务的框架。

Method: 方法上，Text-AB 基于 Diffusion Transformer，并采用 flow-matching 目标训练。在音频表示方面，使用 DAC-VAE 将 48 kHz 波形压缩为 25 Hz 潜在序列，相比 EnCodec 表征实现更高压缩率和更好重建质量。在文本建模方面，直接输入原始文本，通过现成文本编码器与跨注意力机制学习文本—语音对齐，无需强制对齐或显式时长预测。模型规模扩展到 3B 参数，并在 48 万小时单语语音上预训练，随后针对跨语言配音、全双工对话合成和情感全双工对话合成进行监督微调。推理时支持约 1 分钟的一次性生成，并通过 multi-diffusion 实现任意长文本生成，同时使用多阶段重排序提升自动指标质量。

Result: 在真实配音基准上，Text-AB 相比最新内部配音系统在韵律相似度、声音相似度、自然度和可分享性方面取得显著提升。在全双工对话合成中，短对话质量接近真人录音，长对话的人类相似度和表现力明显优于最新内部模型，并能自然建模轮流发言、回应性短反馈和情感动态。在情感对话合成中，情感条件显著提升情感对齐和情感交互质量。

Conclusion: Text-AB 展示了对齐自由潜在扩散语音生成框架在统一配音和对话合成任务中的有效性。通过高效音频潜表示、大规模训练和跨注意力对齐学习，该系统在语音质量、交互自然性、长音频生成和情感表达方面均取得明显进展。

Abstract: We present Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox (Text-AB), a unified framework for high-quality voice dubbing and full-duplex dialogue synthesis. Building on a Diffusion Transformer trained with a flow-matching objective, Text-AB departs from the Audiobox system along three dimensions. First, it operates in a latent diffusion framework using DAC-VAE features that encode 48 kHz waveforms into a 25 Hz latent sequence, giving over 10x higher compression than previous EnCodec representations while improving resynthesis quality. Second, Text-AB is alignment-free: it consumes raw text via an off-the-shelf text encoder and learns text-speech alignment through cross-attention, removing the need for forced alignment and explicit duration prediction. Third, we scale model and data substantially, pretraining a 3B-parameter model on 480k hours of monolingual speech, followed by supervised fine-tuning on three downstream tasks: cross-lingual voice dubbing, full-duplex dialogue synthesis, and emotional full-duplex dialogue synthesis. At inference, Text-AB supports one-shot generation for up to ~1 min of speech and arbitrarily long-form generation via a multi-diffusion scheme, plus a multi-stage reranking strategy that enhances quality based on automated metrics. On a real-world dubbing benchmark, Text-AB delivers a step-change improvement over the latest internal dubbing system, with large gains in prosody similarity, voice similarity, naturalness, and shareability. For full-duplex dialogue synthesis, it approaches human recordings on short-form conversations and substantially outperforms the latest internal model on long-form human-likeness and expressivity, while natively modeling turn-taking, back-channeling, and emotional dynamics. For emotional dialogue synthesis, emotion conditioning significantly improves emotion alignment and emotional interaction quality over the unconditioned baseline.

</details>


### [169] [Representational alignment yields generalizable safety in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04022)
*Lingyu Li,Yan Teng,Yingchun Wang,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在道德概念表征上的不足，并提出一种直接优化潜在表征相似性的对齐方法，以提升模型在对抗条件下的安全鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法主要优化可观察的输出响应，但当相同的不当意图被改写成陌生或对抗形式时，模型仍可能失效。作者希望从原型理论出发，解释并改善模型对道德概念的泛化分类能力。

Method: 作者评估了23个LLM对道德类别及类别内部典型性的表征能力，并提出representational similarity optimization，将模型潜在表示直接对齐到人类道德判断所体现的分类结构；同时与标准行为对齐方法在相同的251,334条道德标注上进行匹配实验。

Result: 实验发现，现有LLM对相反道德类别的区分和类别内部细粒度典型性保留较弱，且这一问题不随参数规模或对齐阶段显著消失。标准行为对齐能提升显式判断表现，但难以改变内部分类结构，并可能增加对抗脆弱性；表征相似性优化在显式判断上提升较温和，但在多种模型规模、基准和攻击策略下更稳定地提升对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于原型的概念分类有助于行为适应性；将这种表征原则迁移到LLM中，可以在对抗条件下带来更具泛化性的安全改进。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) is essential for their safe deployment. Current alignment methods mainly optimize observable responses, yet models remain vulnerable when the same harmful intent is recast in unfamiliar or adversarial forms that humans can easily recognize. Prototype theory offers an account of this adaptability. Human concepts are represented around central cases, and new instances are categorized according to their graded typicality relative to these prototypes. Here we show that such categorization of moral concepts is weakly preserved in current LLMs. Across 23 LLMs, models often failed to distinguish opposed moral categories or preserve fine-grained typicality within each category. These deficits persist across parameter sizes and alignment stages. We developed representational similarity optimization, which directly aligns the latent representations in LLMs with the categorization expressed in human moral judgements, without supervising generated responses. In matched experiments using the same 251,334 moral annotations, standard behavioral alignment learned the intended moral judgements at the response level while leaving the categorization structure largely unchanged and increasing vulnerability across adversarial evaluations. Reorganizing moral categorization produced more modest gains in explicit judgements but consistently improved adversarial robustness across model scales on diverse benchmarks and attack strategies. Our findings provide functional support for the view that prototype-based categorization contributes to behavioral adaptability. They also show that transferring this representational principle to LLMs yields generalizable safety under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [170] [Translation as a Decision Space: A Multi-Agent Perspective on Low-Resource Dialect Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04048)
*Hasan Alkhder,Mohammad Abboush,Igor Tchappi,Ahmet Zengin,Amro Najjar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) systems typically produce a single output per input, obscuring the alternative decision trajectories implicitly available within multilingual decoding. This opacity becomes particularly problematic in low-resource dialect settings, where multiple linguistically valid realizations may differ in lexical authenticity, register, and structural stability. We propose reframing translation as a structured decision space explored by autonomous translation agents. Instead of analyzing a single output, we model distinct translation pathways as agents operating over a shared multilingual backbone. Inter-agent divergence is treated not as error but as an interpretable behavioral signal. We conduct an empirical study on Turkish--Syrian Arabic translation using three agents: (1) zero-shot direct translation, (2) dialect-stabilized translation via lightweight fine-tuning, and (3) pivot translation through English. Evaluation is performed on 5,000 dialogue sentences, while stabilization is trained on 5,000 additional Turkish--Syrian sentence pairs drawn from television dialogue and MADAR-Turk resources. Rather than optimizing for conventional performance metrics, we quantify structured behavioral displacement using dialect marker frequency, lexical proximity to standardized Arabic, and structural variance. Lightweight stabilization nearly doubles dialect marker usage, increasing it from 0.2266 to 0.4988, while significantly reducing structural instability. Pivot mediation introduces normalization pressure and measurable compression effects, whereas zero-shot translation exhibits the highest decision variance. We argue that translation divergence across agents reveals latent decision flexibility within multilingual models and we provide a principled interpretability framework for low-resource dialect generation.

</details>


### [171] [Sequential Beats Joint: On the Interplay between On-Policy Distillation and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04108)
*Boyan Li,Bingsen Chen,Chenghao Yang,Ping Nie,Chen Zhao,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种两阶段后训练范式：先进行 on-policy distillation（OPD），再进行 reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards（RLVR）。该方法在逻辑与数学推理基准上优于纯 OPD、纯 RLVR 以及联合优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将 OPD 的密集 token 级监督与 RLVR 的稀疏奖励在同一步中融合，但作者认为两类信号在联合优化时可能相互干扰，需要探索更有效的组合方式。

Method: 采用简单的两阶段流程 OPD-then-RL：第一阶段用 OPD 扩大学生模型对教师支持解法的覆盖；第二阶段用 RLVR 在已有支持空间内进一步强化高质量推理。论文还通过 pass@$k$ 行为、学习动态和参数更新分析解释其有效性，并研究何时从 OPD 切换到 RL。

Result: OPD-then-RL 在逻辑和数学推理 benchmark 上持续超过纯 OPD、纯 RLVR、加权相加融合、教师调制优势缩放等联合基线。实验还发现 OPD 验证分数是切换到 RL 的关键指标，且 OPD 比 SFT 更适合作为 RL 冷启动。

Conclusion: 将 OPD 与 RLVR 解耦为连续阶段比在单步中联合优化更有效；OPD 负责扩展可行解覆盖，RLVR 负责在该覆盖内锐化性能。OPD-then-RL 是一种简单、稳定且强的后训练推理 LLM 方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have emerged as two dominant methods for post-training reasoning LLMs. Prior work uses OPD's dense token-level supervision to complement the sparse RL reward, fusing the two signals within a single step: either as a \emph{weighted-additive combination} or a \emph{teacher-modulated rescaling} of the RL advantage. In this paper, we show that a simple two-stage scheme, OPD-then-RL, consistently outperforms pure OPD, pure RLVR, and all such joint baselines across logic and math reasoning benchmarks. Beyond the empirical results, we further provide a systematic understanding of this through pass@$k$ behavior, learning dynamics, and parameter updates, yielding a consistent explanation: OPD expands the student's coverage of teacher-supported solutions and RL sharpens within that support, while jointly optimizing the two signals causes them to interfere.To provide a practical recipe, we find that the OPD validation score is the key signal for when to switch to RL, and that OPD is a better cold start for RL than SFT. Together, our results establish OPD-then-RL as a simple yet strong way to combine the two methods, turning two entangled signals into complementary stages.

</details>


### [172] [Last Translation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04173)
*Vilém Zouhar,Niyati Bafna,Mukund Choudhary,Maike Züfle,Sara Rajaee,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Sara Papi,Ona de Gibert,Bhavitvya Malik,Eliya Habba,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Patrícia Schmidtová,Michelle Wastl,Sheriff Issaka,Leshem Choshen,Stella Biderman,Antonis Anastasopoulos,Jan Niehues,Rico Sennrich,Mrinmaya Sachan,Ondřej Bojar,Kenton Murray,Jörg Tiedemann,Alham Fikri Aji,Philipp Koehn,Christof Monz,Alexandra Birch,Sowmya Vajjala,Chalamalasetti Kranti,Cristina España-Bonet,Nobin Sarwar,David Kaczér,Shunta Asano,Malik Marmonier,Daban Q. Jaff,Vaisakhi Mishra,Hend Al- Khalifa,Gabriele Sarti,Sourajit Saha,Nils Rehlinger,Juan Daniel Cuervo Villa,Jonathan Tonglet,Saugata Purkayastha,Dominik Macháček,Jagannathan Ramanujam,Heejin Do,Zuzana Nadova,Fred Philippy,Fabian Retkowski,Maria Lymperaiou,Silvia Casola,Hanna Yukhymenko,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Sangwon Ryu,Andrés Jerez,Ron Keinan,Shuaib Shuaib Yusuf,Avantica Vempati,Maria Carmen Staiano,Sukannya Purkayastha,Adrian Cosma,Vitalii Babenko,Erivan Inan,Aviral Nigam,Wafa Aissa,Fatima Haouari,Venkata Prasanth Kumar Gummadi,Mehdi Jafarzadeh,Valentin Scourneau,Lukas Edman,Kaiser Sun,Shaomu Tan,Mohammad Sadegh Gholizadeh,Johannes-Rudolf David,Dipankar Srirag,Javier García Gilabert,Ruta Binkyte,Manar Ali,Ana-Maria Bucur,Sabry E. Farrag,Youssef Saber,Yihong Liu,Jean Maillard,Cojocaru Nicoleta,Xiaochuang Yuan,Sina Ahmadi,Philipp Mondorf,Kaustubh Dhole,Roman Wixinger,Shenbin Qian,Manuel Tuor,Sergey Troshin,Jonathan Yahav,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Amir Arsalan Rezapour,Lance Calvin Lim Gamboa,Manon Reusens,Kätriin Kukk,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Giuseppe Gallipoli,Christian Hoang,Shaswati Saha,Seth Aycock,Jan Kocoń,Bo Chen,Linh Vu,Vatsal Venkatkrishna,Arafat Ahsan,Luan Thanh Nguyen,Hassan Soliman,Daryna Dementieva,Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Ngoc Quynh Tram Do,Marius Huber,Kazuki Egashira,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Vladislav Poritski,Mike Zhang,Deep Shah,Paul Gavrikov,Luis Frentzen Salim,David Africa,R. Damanhuri,Bello Umar Bello,Anumit Garg,Gengyu Rao,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Kamile Dementaviciute,Andrianos Michail,L D M S Sai Teja,Dawei Zhu,Yi Fan,Wei Liu,Farhan Farsi,Elias Herranen,Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Karen Sanchez,Farzad Shami,Ashok Urlana,Zimu Wang,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Priyaranjan Pattnayak,Marii Ojastu,Hongbin Na,Emilian Radoi,Chenyi Zhao,Carlos Hinojosa,Andrea Gregor de Varda,Zaid Alyafeai,Reem Alzahrani,Nehal Kathrotia,Alex Flückiger,Ulysses Sekai Tully Carr,Jimson Paulo Layacan,Guy Kaplan,Ritwik Tiwari,Rishit Dagli,Oksana Volchek,Isaac R Caswell,Bowen Yi,Blanka Kövér,Amir Hossein Yari,Aicha Chorana,Zhengxiang Wang,Selja Keränen,Samuel Simko,Joy Olusanya,Jenny Chim,Enzo Doyen,Vivek Harsha Lakkamaneni,Sophia Conrad,Pouya Sadeghi,Panayiotis Panayiotou,Luis Lara,Jannatul Nayem,Eran Yahav,Debanshu Das,Antonia Karamolegkou,Anmol Goel,Aishik Mandal,Tommaso Cerruti,Raoyuan Zhao,Mykola Haltiuk,Thura Aung,Naser Almousa,Amir Hossein Kargaran,Rachel Bawden,Qiaoyuan Zheng,Mateusz Lango,Beni Egressy,Fidel Rodríguez Velásquez,Natchapon Jongwiriyanurak,Minh Ngoc Do,Marco Gaido,Lena Libon,Dzmitry Kuzmin,Badal Nyalang,Antoine Taroni,Andrei Niculae,Abdulaziz Nura Kani,Rushikesh Zawar,Marek Šuppa,Beatrice Savoldi,Andreas Simons,Rayyan Merchant,Ilai Yaron Levy,Francesco Pinto,Ziyi Yang,Yolanda Xavier,Samuel Frontull,Muhammad Ravi Shulthan Habibi,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Harris Abdul Majid,Francesca Padovani,Tim Graf,Tatiana Bielakova,Sharifa Djurabaeva,Shaoxiong Ji,Raia Abu Ahmad,Pavel Stepachev,Jirui Qi,Ayush Sunil Munot,Alireza Pakniat,Ayla Rigouts Terryn,Yuxing Lu,Yurii Paniv,Xiyan Fu,Tosin Adewumi,Sunisth Kumar,Stéphane J. P. S. Thunus,Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish,Shayan Bali,Prakhar Gupta,Papa Abdou Karim Karou Diallo,Matija Akrap,Marko Culjak,Kristýna Onderková,Joseph Attieh,Esrael Teferi Tensay,Elisabeth Fittschen,Benoît Sagot,Jingwei Ni,Yu Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Last Translation Benchmark（LTB），一个持续更新的多模态机器翻译评测集，专门收集能使领先翻译模型失败的样例，并为每个样例配套人工设计的验证规则，以实现更可靠、可复现、可操作的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器翻译基准逐渐饱和，难以区分先进模型能力；自动评价指标不可靠、易被“刷分”且难以指出具体问题；人工评价虽有价值，但常存在可复现性、客观性和规模化不足的问题。因此领域需要能揭示模型失败边界并指导改进的新型评测方法。

Method: 作者构建由人工撰写、同行评审的多模态样例集合，覆盖文本、图像、音频、视频等形式，目标是挑战当前领先机器翻译模型。每个样例都附带手工编写的验证规则，用于明确描述该样例上的具体失败条件，从而替代单一分数式评估。该数据集设计为持续接收贡献的动态基准。

Result: 最新版本 LTBv1 收录了截至 2026 年 9 月 1 日前通过审核的贡献，并计划随着新数据持续收集而发布后续版本。摘要未给出具体模型评测分数或实验对比结果，但强调该基准能够系统暴露领先模型的失败案例。

Conclusion: LTB 试图将机器翻译评测从饱和、不可解释的指标驱动模式，转向基于具体失败规则、可复现且可操作的诊断式评估，有助于更客观地追踪领域进展并发现改进路径。

Abstract: For scientific progress, we need benchmarks that test the limits of state-of-the-art models, and evaluation methods that inform us about failure cases. As models get stronger, standard benchmarks for machine translation are approaching saturation. Further, automatic translation metrics are unreliable, vulnerable to reward-hacking, and provide unactionable assessments. Even gold human evaluation is not problem-free, because it often lacks reproducibility, objectivity, and scalability. Overall, this prevents us from tracking objective progress in the field and identifying pathways for improvement. We introduce the Last Translation Benchmark, a collection of human-authored and peer-reviewed examples (texts, images, audio, videos) that break leading machine translation models. We also present a new evaluation approach: each example comes with handcrafted verification rules describing concrete failure cases on that example, therefore allowing reliable and actionable future evaluation. The Last Translation Benchmark is a live dataset that accepts ongoing contributions. The latest version is LTBv1, containing accepted contributions prior to September 1st 2026, with future releases planned as new data is continuously collected.

</details>


### [173] [Knowledge Acquisition During Pre-training? Large Language Models Learn Better With Auxiliary Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04180)
*Joseph Lee,Yidi Huang,Dokyoon Kim,Shu Yang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型预训练中知识获取的机制，发现知识的辅助视图（如改写、补充表述）能在固定 token 预算下提升学习效果，并解释数据多样性为何重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对 LLM 在预训练阶段如何获得知识仍理解不足。作者关注一个核心问题：同一知识的不同表述或辅助信息是否不仅是相关因素，而是对模型学习具有因果帮助。

Method: 作者设计受控实验来隔离辅助视图的作用：比较重复、释义、文档重复与辅助视图分配等条件；在固定 token 预算下评估知识学习与事实回忆；考察生成辅助视图的教师模型强弱影响；分析不同知识类型，并通过逐层偏置与压缩现象进行机制层面的解释。

Result: 实验表明：重复对知识习得是必要的；释义主要在较小 batch size 下更有帮助；在固定 token 预算下，将部分重复 token 替换为辅助视图能提升学习，甚至对事实记忆也有效；辅助视图效果不依赖强教师模型；上下文性知识与基础性知识能在已有知识缺口下促进学习。

Conclusion: 辅助知识表征是 LLM 预训练成功的重要因素之一。它们在大规模语料中自然存在，能够提升知识学习效率，并为“数据多样性为何有助于预训练”提供了合理解释。

Abstract: Gaps remain in our understanding of how large language models (LLMs) acquire knowledge during pre-training. We posit that auxiliary views, reformulations of knowledge, are causally helpful for learning. We design controlled experiments to isolate this. First, we confirm that repetition is necessary for acquisition and clarify that paraphrasing helps only at smaller batch sizes. Second, holding the token budget fixed, allocating tokens from document repetition to auxiliary views improves learning, counterintuitively, even for factual recall. Third, the effectiveness of auxiliary views is not contingent on the strength of the teacher model that generates them. Fourth, we identify forms of knowledge, contextual and foundational, that aid learning in the presence of prior knowledge gaps. Finally, we examine how these effects manifest mechanistically via layer-wise biases and compression. Together, our findings suggest that auxiliary representations of knowledge, which arise naturally in large pre-training corpora, are a key factor in the success of pre-training and offer a plausible explanation for why data diversity matters.

</details>


### [174] [Legibility is Not Interpretability: Comparing Judged and Actual Importance in Chain-Of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04194)
*Kevin Du,Alexander Hoyle,Laura Ruis,Acyr Locatelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究链式思维推理文本是否真的包含“哪些步骤重要”的信息；结果表明，大模型评审能部分识别重要步骤，但远未达到噪声上限，说明推理轨迹的可读性不等同于可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多工作把链式思维模型的推理轨迹当作理解模型答案形成过程的窗口，并用 LLM 评审、过程奖励模型和生成式批评器来诊断错误、评估忠实性或提供逐步监督。该论文质疑一个关键前提：推理步骤的文本本身是否编码了其功能重要性。

Method: 作者将某个推理步骤的重要性定义为其 advantage，即包含该步骤相对于不包含该步骤时对期望奖励（例如最终答案正确率）的改变，并通过 Monte Carlo rollouts 估计该值作为近似真值。随后评估 LLM judges 是否能识别高 advantage 的步骤，并进一步微调模型作为步骤级 critic 来测试可恢复性。

Result: 能力较强的 LLM 评审能够超过简单的 prevalence baseline，但明显低于 noise ceiling。微调后的步骤级 critic 在错误回答场景中有显著提升，但在正确回答场景中仍与上限存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 推理步骤的重要性只能从推理文本中部分恢复。论文提醒研究者不要把链式思维文本的可读性直接等同于模型内部推理的可解释性，这对过程奖励建模和基于推理轨迹的监督方法具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.

</details>


### [175] [ESPO: Error-Structured Prompt Optimization via Diagnose, Diversify, and Stabilize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04197)
*Lihao Liu,Peng Tang,Kunwar Yashraj Singh,Shabnam Ghadar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ESPO 将提示词优化拆解为诊断、生成和选择三阶段，在多项 NLP 基准上比 GEPA 更准确、更短且推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化式提示词优化器（如 GEPA）存在 prompt bloat：迭代中不断追加规则和例外，导致提示词显著变长却没有带来相应准确率提升。作者将问题归因于错误观察不完整、搜索多样性不足和候选选择不可靠。

Method: 提出 ESPO（Error-Structured Prompt Optimization）：首先 Diagnose 一次性聚类训练错误并抽取结构性错误模式；随后 Propose 用四种互补策略生成具有不同偏置的候选提示词；最后 Select 使用 bootstrap stability selection 进行稳健选择。论文还给出泛化界，将三阶段分别对应到测试时性能差距的相关项。

Result: 在 Tweet、MMLU、GSM8K、HotpotQA、ScoNe、HoVer、PUPA 七个公开基准上，ESPO 平均准确率为 74.67%，比 GEPA 的 70.91% 高 3.76 个百分点；每个数据集上均匹配或超过 GEPA，同时提示词长度短 47%（1,004 vs 1,878 字符）并提升推理速度。跨 Gemma 3 12B、Mistral 14B、Qwen3 32B、Claude Haiku 4.5 的实验中，ESPO 在所有模型上平均准确率最佳；Qwen3 GSM8K 上从 15.00% 提升到 91.40%。消融显示，仅增加多样性而不使用 bootstrap 选择会使性能下降 1.20%。

Conclusion: ESPO 通过结构化错误诊断、多样化候选生成和稳健选择，缓解了提示词膨胀问题，并在准确率、提示词长度和推理效率之间取得更优平衡；其理论分析和消融实验共同支持三阶段设计的必要性。

Abstract: Evolutionary prompt optimizers such as GEPA suffer from prompt bloat: each iteration appends rules and caveats, producing prompts up to 3$\times$ longer yet no more accurate.
